Windows proof (A34; PSC-25 *Windows (A34)*, PS-2.9) -- platform-sbom, unit U3 (PSC-13; security L-2)
HEAD: 98b6d27c1f735603b581b7238df43475896495a0 (agent/platform-sbom) PLUS the uncommitted U3 working tree
(docker-compose.sbom.yml `user: ${KE_SBOM_USER:?}` and header; scripts/bundle.sh SBOM section; scripts/sbom_check.py;
scripts/offline_check.sh; tests); host aria-alien1, Windows 11 Pro 10.0.26200, PowerShell 7.6.7 (Core; Windows
PowerShell 5.1.26100.8655 also present), Docker Desktop 4.75.0, Engine 29.5.2 (linux/amd64), Compose v5.1.4, Git Bash
5.3.9 (MINGW64_NT-10.0-26200). Date 2026-10-23 (UTC), tester.
Why: U3 changes docker-compose.sbom.yml (`user:` and the header) and makes bundle.sh run syft for real, with Windows-host
fixes (hostpath/cygpath -m, MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1, mkdir then chmod); under A34 a compose-file change needs a recorded
Windows run before the unit commits. The sha256 of docker/app.Dockerfile, docker-compose.sbom.yml, scripts/bundle.sh,
scripts/offline_check.sh, scripts/sbom_check.py, uv.lock and pyproject.toml is recorded at start (Git Bash, 00-start;
and section 0, PowerShell) and at the end (final check): identical, so the proof is tied to this exact tree; if any of
those files changes before commit, the proof is stale.
Template: docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u2/windows-run.txt (same driver, same shim, same phases and order: pre, build,
up, down, tests; then verify fast and the final check from Git Bash). The U2 driver and shim were taken verbatim from
the end of that file. Differences from U2, on purpose:
  - own project COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=ke-tester-u3win and own image tag KE_IMAGE_TAG=u3win-98b6d27 in the driver's
    process environment (and exported in section 3b); the 'local' tag was never used or moved;
  - .env = .env.example plus one comment marker line (U3WIN-THROWAWAY; no real keys; KE_MODEL_UPSTREAM empty);
    .env did not exist before (00-start, section 1) and was removed after (section 8, final check);
  - section 0 hashes seven files (U2's six plus scripts/sbom_check.py);
  - section 2b extended: KE_SBOM_USER=1000:1000 (dummy) set with the dummy KE_SBOM_IN/OUT, the resolved config shows
    `user: 1000:1000`; NEW fail-closed with KE_SBOM_USER unset (exit 1 naming KE_SBOM_USER); the U2 KE_SBOM_IN-unset
    case repeated with KE_SBOM_USER set. The U2 "ports: 1 entries" artifact (@($null).Count) is gone: key presence is
    now read from the property names. The "set but empty" case is run from Git Bash in 3b (PowerShell's $env:X = ''
    removes the variable, so it cannot be told apart from unset there);
  - NEW section 3b (Git Bash, between phases build and up, script <scratch>/bundle-u3.sh copied at the end):
    scripts/bundle.sh --out <scratch>/bundle-u3win-amd64 once, with the images phase build tagged (build.ps1,
    KE_IMAGE_TAG=u3win-98b6d27). Before the run: freshness of both first-party images (security M-1, coordinator
    addition): ke-app:u3win-98b6d27 = sha256:2becf293d345..., no psycopg_binary, no pip, psycopg impl c 150019,
    dpkg libpq5 15.19-0+deb12u1; the stale ke-app:local (510c1b59b838, has psycopg_binary) shown for contrast only.
    After: the six SBOMs' sizes, MANIFEST verified, validate() per file, then the scratch bundle deleted;
  - bundle.sh `docker tag`s ke-app/ke-gateway:bundle-98b6d27c1f73 (which existed before, on the developer's stale
    :local images) to the tester's images; section 3b put both back on their earlier image IDs (510c1b59..., ebdf7b07...)
    and the final check shows them unchanged; the ke-vendor tags were re-applied to the same IDs (no change);
  - FINDING (host prerequisite, not a code change): bundle.sh needs jq on PATH. On this host jq 1.8.2 is installed by
    winget but is not on the Git Bash PATH, and native jq.exe writes CRLF by default (3b shows `b \r \n`), so every
    `$(jq -er ...)` capture in bundle.sh would end in \r and fail ref_re. The run used <scratch>/bin/jq, a two-line
    wrapper running that jq.exe with -b (binary, LF output; jq's documented option for native jq.exe under
    MSYS2/Cygwin). The developer's earlier run (u3/bundle-run.txt) does not record which jq it used. Docs for a
    Windows build host should name this (orchestrator/documenter);
  - section 3b attempt 1 aborted on a tester bug (its output filter's sed expression for the Windows scratch path
    failed, "Invalid back reference", so every step got SIGPIPE and the script hung on jq.exe writing to the broken
    pipe); it was killed before scripts/bundle.sh started: nothing was written, no container, no work dir (checked).
    Its 18 lines are kept at the end. Attempt 2, with a perl literal-match filter, is section 3b;
  - the build was served from the BuildKit layer cache (about 4 s); build.ps1 ran end to end with exit 0.
Driver: <scratch>\winproof-u3.ps1 (copied at the end), run from Git Bash as
  pwsh -NoProfile -File <scratch>\winproof-u3.ps1 -Phase <pre|build|up|down|tests>
with NO -ExecutionPolicy flag; each repo script ran as  pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\<x>.ps1
Safety shim: <scratch>\shim\docker.cmd -> shim.ps1 (copied at the end; the U2 shim, comment line changed).
Redaction: the profile path is shown as <profile> and the session scratch directory as <scratch> throughout (one
literal-match pass over this whole file; the Git Bash `$ ...` echo lines included); no SID occurred (grep for the user-SID prefix, 'S-1-5-' then '21', over the whole file: 0 hits).
The same pass also rewrote the path literals inside the copied red() filter of bundle-u3.sh (shown as
s#<profile>#<profile>#), so that one listed line differs from the script as run. Usernames and the host name are kept. Otherwise output is unedited; the "####"
separators, this header, the verdict and the section headers for 00-start, 3b, the final check and verify fast were
added by the tester.
Working tree: git status --short identical at start (00-start), section 8 and the final check, apart from the files

# U3 (PSC-13, security L-2) Windows proof driver (tester). The U2 driver (docs/evidence/platform-sbom/u2/windows-run.txt) with project, tag, marker and hash list changed,
# section 2b extended (KE_SBOM_USER, `user:`, USER fail-closed); sections 4b and 4c kept. Section 3b (bundle.sh) runs from Git Bash between phases build and up.
# Own project and image tag so no other project or image is touched.
param([Parameter(Mandatory)][ValidateSet('pre', 'build', 'up', 'down', 'tests')][string]$Phase)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
$PSStyle.OutputRendering = 'PlainText'
$repo = 'C:\repos\Knowledge-engine'
$scratch = Split-Path -Parent $PSCommandPath
$shimDir = Join-Path $scratch 'shim'
$envBak = Join-Path $scratch 'env.bak'
$project = 'ke-tester-u3win'
$tag = 'u3win-98b6d27'
Set-Location $repo
$env:COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME = $project
$env:KE_IMAGE_TAG = $tag
$filter = "label=com.docker.compose.project=$project"

function Section([string]$t) { ''; "==================== $t ===================="; '' }
function Step([string]$label, [scriptblock]$sb) {
    "PS> $label"
    $global:LASTEXITCODE = $null
    & $sb 2>&1 | ForEach-Object { "$_" }
    "[exit code: $global:LASTEXITCODE]"
    ''
}
function EnvDiff {
    Compare-Object (Get-Content .env.example) (Get-Content .env) |
        ForEach-Object { '{0} {1}' -f $(if ($_.SideIndicator -eq '=>') { '+ .env        ' } else { '- .env.example' }), $_.InputObject }
}
function ProjectPorts {
    docker ps --filter $filter --format '{{.Names}}  {{.Ports}}'
}

$pyImpl = 'import psycopg; print(psycopg.pq.__impl__, psycopg.pq.version())'
$pyMaps = 'import importlib.util as u, psycopg; psycopg.pq.version(); print("psycopg_c.pq:", u.find_spec("psycopg_c.pq").origin); print("libpq mapped:", sorted({l.split()[-1] for l in open("/proc/self/maps") if "libpq" in l})); print("psycopg_binary spec:", u.find_spec("psycopg_binary"))'
$pyPip = "import importlib.util as u; print(u.find_spec('pip'))"
$shLdd = 'for f in /opt/venv/lib/python3*/site-packages/psycopg_c/pq*.so; do echo "$f"; ldd "$f" | grep -E "libpq|psycopg_binary"; done'

    Step 'pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\launch.ps1' { pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\launch.ps1 }
    Step 'docker compose ps' { docker compose ps }
    Step 'docker compose ps --format json (service/state/health)' { docker compose ps --format json | ForEach-Object { $o = $_ | ConvertFrom-Json; '{0,-10} {1,-8} {2}' -f $o.Service, $o.State, $o.Health } }
    Step 'Invoke-WebRequest http://127.0.0.1:47100/healthz' { $r = Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing http://127.0.0.1:47100/healthz; "$($r.StatusCode) $($r.Content)" }
    Step 'curl.exe -sS -i http://127.0.0.1:47100/readyz' { curl.exe -sS -i http://127.0.0.1:47100/readyz }
    Step 'Invoke-WebRequest http://127.0.0.1:47101/ (Phoenix UI title)' { $r = Invoke-WebRequest -UseBasicParsing http://127.0.0.1:47101/; "$($r.StatusCode) " + ([regex]::Match($r.Content, '<title>[^<]*</title>').Value) }
    Step "docker port $project-gateway-1" { docker port "$project-gateway-1" }
    Step 'project containers and published ports' { ProjectPorts }
    Step 'Get-NetTCPConnection -State Listen -LocalPort 47100,47101 (host listeners)' { Get-NetTCPConnection -State Listen -LocalPort 47100, 47101 -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue | ForEach-Object { '{0}:{1}' -f $_.LocalAddress, $_.LocalPort } | Sort-Object -Unique }
    Step 'TCP connect <this host non-loopback IPv4, not recorded>:47100 within 3 s (must be False)' {
        $ip = (Get-NetIPAddress -AddressFamily IPv4 | Where-Object { $_.IPAddress -notlike '127.*' -and $_.IPAddress -notlike '169.254.*' -and $_.InterfaceAlias -notlike 'vEthernet*' } | Select-Object -First 1).IPAddress
        $c = [Net.Sockets.TcpClient]::new()
        $ok = $false
        try { $ok = $c.ConnectAsync($ip, 47100).Wait(3000) -and $c.Connected } catch { $ok = $false } finally { $c.Dispose() }
        "connected: $ok"
    }

    Section "4b. (PSC-25/U0, repeated): psycopg implementation and libpq in the launched api (project $project)"
    Step "docker inspect $project-api-1 --format '{{.Config.Image}} {{.Image}}'" { docker inspect "$project-api-1" --format '{{.Config.Image}} {{.Image}}' }
    Step "docker compose exec -T api python -c `"$pyImpl`"" { docker compose exec -T api python -c $pyImpl }
    Step 'docker compose exec -T api python -I -c <psycopg_c.pq origin; libpq files mapped in /proc/self/maps; psycopg_binary spec>' { docker compose exec -T api python -I -c $pyMaps }
    Step 'docker compose exec -T api sh -c ''for f in /opt/venv/lib/python3*/site-packages/psycopg_c/pq*.so; do echo "$f"; ldd "$f" | grep -E "libpq|psycopg_binary"; done''' { docker compose exec -T api sh -c $shLdd }
    Step "docker compose exec -T api dpkg-query -W -f='`${Package} `${Version}\n' libpq5" { docker compose exec -T api dpkg-query -W -f='${Package} ${Version}\n' libpq5 }
    Step "docker compose exec -T worker python -c `"$pyImpl`" (worker shares ke-app)" { docker compose exec -T worker python -c $pyImpl }

    Section "4c. (PSC-14, N10a/U1, repeated): no pip in the launched api; flattened ke-app (project $project)"
    Step "docker compose exec -T api python -c `"$pyPip`" (must print None)" { docker compose exec -T api python -c $pyPip }
    Step "docker compose exec -T api /usr/local/bin/python3 -I -c `"$pyPip`" (must print None)" { docker compose exec -T api /usr/local/bin/python3 -I -c $pyPip }
    Step 'docker compose exec -T api ls /usr/local/lib/python3.12/ensurepip/_bundled/ (no pip wheel)' { docker compose exec -T api ls /usr/local/lib/python3.12/ensurepip/_bundled/ }
    Step 'docker compose exec -T api ls -la /usr/local/lib/python3.12/ensurepip/_bundled/ (same, long form)' { docker compose exec -T api ls -la /usr/local/lib/python3.12/ensurepip/_bundled/ }
    Step "docker compose exec -T worker python -c `"$pyPip`" (worker shares ke-app; must print None)" { docker compose exec -T worker python -c $pyPip }
    Step "docker image history ke-app:$tag" { docker image history "ke-app:$tag" }
    Step "docker image history --no-trunc --format '{{.Size}} | {{.CreatedBy}}' ke-app:$tag" { docker image history --no-trunc --format '{{.Size}} | {{.CreatedBy}}' "ke-app:$tag" }
    Step "docker image inspect ke-app:$tag --format '<RootFS layer count>; <Config.Env>'" { docker image inspect "ke-app:$tag" --format '{{len .RootFS.Layers}} rootfs layers; Env={{json .Config.Env}}' }

    Section '5. Safety shim verification (before the refusal case)'
    $shimLog = Join-Path $scratch 'shim.log'
    if (Test-Path $shimLog) { Remove-Item $shimLog }
    $env:KE_SHIM_LOG = $shimLog
    $realPath = $env:PATH
    $env:PATH = "$shimDir;$realPath"
    Step 'shim: Get-Command docker (resolves to the shim)' { (Get-Command docker).Source -replace [regex]::Escape($scratch), '<scratch>' }
    Step "shim: docker version --format '{{.Server.Version}}' (passes through)" { docker version --format '{{.Server.Version}}' }
    Step 'shim: docker compose up --help (must be intercepted, exit 99; harmless if not)' { docker compose up --help | Select-Object -First 2 }
    Step 'shim call log' { Get-Content $shimLog }
    Remove-Item $shimLog

    Section '5a. Refusal: KE_BIND_ADDRESS=0.0.0.0 and KE_ENV=prod in .env (launch.ps1 under the shim)'
    (Get-Content .env) -replace '^KE_BIND_ADDRESS=.*$', 'KE_BIND_ADDRESS=0.0.0.0' -replace '^KE_ENV=.*$', 'KE_ENV=prod' | Set-Content .env
    Step 'diff .env.example .env' { EnvDiff }
    Step 'pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\launch.ps1 (under the shim)' { pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\launch.ps1 }
    Step 'shim call log (no compose up)' { Get-Content $shimLog; ''; "compose up reached: $([bool](Get-Content $shimLog | Where-Object { $_ -match '\bcompose\b' -and $_ -match '\bup\b' }))" }
    $env:PATH = $realPath
    Remove-Item Env:KE_SHIM_LOG
    Step 'project ports (unchanged, 127.0.0.1 only)' { ProjectPorts }
    Step 'Restore .env from <scratch>\env.bak; diff .env.example .env' { Copy-Item $envBak .env -Force; EnvDiff }
}
'down' {
    Section '6. scripts\teardown.ps1 (no -Volumes: containers and networks go, named volumes stay)'
    Step "volumes before: docker volume ls --filter $filter" { docker volume ls --filter $filter }
    Step 'pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\teardown.ps1' { pwsh -NoProfile -File scripts\teardown.ps1 }
    Step "docker ps -a --filter $filter" { docker ps -a --filter $filter }
    Step "docker network ls --filter $filter" { docker network ls --filter $filter }
    Step "docker volume ls --filter $filter (kept)" { docker volume ls --filter $filter }

    Section '7. scripts\teardown.ps1 -Volumes (the tester removes its own project volumes)'

    Step "docker ps -a --filter $filter" { docker ps -a --filter $filter }
    Step "docker network ls --filter $filter" { docker network ls --filter $filter }
    Step "docker volume ls --filter $filter" { docker volume ls --filter $filter }
    Step "docker volume ls --filter name=${project}_" { docker volume ls --filter "name=${project}_" }
    Step "docker compose ls -a --filter name=$project" { docker compose ls -a --filter "name=$project" }

    Section '8. Remove temporary .env and the tester-owned image tags'
    Step 'Remove-Item .env, <scratch>\env.bak; Test-Path .env' { Remove-Item .env, $envBak; Test-Path .env }
    Step "docker image rm ke-app:$tag ke-gateway:$tag ke-gates:$tag (untag only; tester-owned tags)" { docker image rm "ke-app:$tag" "ke-gateway:$tag" "ke-gates:$tag" }
    Step "docker image ls --filter reference=ke-*:$tag" { docker image ls --filter "reference=ke-*:$tag" }
    Step 'git status --short' { git status --short }
}
'tests' {
    Section '9. Windows-only tests (skipped in the Linux gates image; run on this host)'
    $env:HARNESS_TRACING = 'off'
    Remove-Item Env:COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME, Env:KE_IMAGE_TAG

    }

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/shim/docker.cmd ====================
@pwsh -NoProfile -File "%~dp0shim.ps1" %*

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/shim/shim.ps1 ====================
# Safety shim (tester, U3 Windows proof; copied from the U2 shim): logs each docker call, refuses any `docker compose ... up` with exit 99, passes the rest to the real docker.exe.
if ($env:KE_SHIM_LOG) { Add-Content -Path $env:KE_SHIM_LOG -Value ($args -join ' ') }
if (($args -contains 'compose') -and ($args -contains 'up')) { Write-Output 'shim: intercepted docker compose up (exit 99)'; exit 99 }
& 'C:\Program Files\Docker\Docker\resources\bin\docker.exe' @args
exit $LASTEXITCODE

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/bin/jq ====================

# tester wrapper: the winget jq.exe in binary mode (-b, LF output), its documented use under MSYS2/Git Bash

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/bundle-u3.sh ====================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Section 3b (tester, U3 Windows proof): scripts/bundle.sh once from Git Bash on this host, to a scratch --out.
set -u
S=<scratch>
R=/c/repos/Knowledge-engine
SM="$(cygpath -m "$S")"; SW="$(cygpath -w "$S")"
export RED_S="$S" RED_SM="$SM" RED_SW="$SW"
red() { perl -pe 's/\Q$ENV{RED_S}\E/<scratch>/gi; s/\Q$ENV{RED_SM}\E/<scratch>/gi; s/\Q$ENV{RED_SW}\E/<scratch>/gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi; s#<profile>#<profile>#gi'; }
run() { echo "\$ $1"; bash -c "$1" 2>&1 | red; echo "[exit code: ${PIPESTATUS[0]}]"; echo; }
sec() { echo; echo "==================== $1 ===================="; echo; }
cd "$R"
export COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=ke-tester-u3win KE_IMAGE_TAG=u3win-98b6d27
export PATH="$S/bin:$PATH"
OUT="$S/bundle-u3win-amd64"
SHA12="$(git rev-parse HEAD | cut -c1-12)"

sec "3b. NEW (PSC-13/U3): scripts/bundle.sh once from Git Bash on this Windows host, to a scratch --out (hostpath/cygpath, MSYS_NO_PATHCONV, mkdir then chmod)"
echo "date (UTC): $(date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ)"
echo "COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME=$COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME KE_IMAGE_TAG=$KE_IMAGE_TAG (exported, as build.ps1 tagged the images in phase build); --out <scratch>/bundle-u3win-amd64"
echo
run 'bash --version | head -1; uname -s -r; command -v cygpath docker'
echo '--- jq: bundle.sh needs it on PATH; the host has jq 1.8.2 from winget, not on the Git Bash PATH, and native jq.exe writes CRLF:'
run 'J=<profile>/AppData/Local/Microsoft/WinGet/Packages/jqlang.jq_Microsoft.Winget.Source_8wekyb3d8bbwe/jq.exe; "$J" --version; printf "%s" "{\"a\":\"b\"}" | "$J" -er .a | od -c | head -1; sha256sum "$J"'
echo '--- so <scratch>/bin/jq (on PATH for this section only) runs it with -b, LF output (jq manual: for native jq.exe under MSYS2/Cygwin):'
run 'cat "$(command -v jq)"; command -v jq; jq --version; printf "%s" "{\"a\":\"b\"}" | jq -er .a | od -c | head -1'
run 'echo "id -u:id -g = $(id -u):$(id -g) (the KE_SBOM_USER bundle.sh passes)"'

echo '--- fail-closed from Git Bash (U3 L-2): KE_SBOM_USER empty, and unset, in the form bundle.sh reads the ref with'
run 'KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER= docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom'
run 'env -u KE_SBOM_USER KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom'
run 'KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER="$(id -u):$(id -g)" docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --images sbom'

echo '--- before'
run "ls -d \"$OUT\" \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\"  # must not exist"
run 'ls -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}"/ke-sbom.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l  # bundle.sh work dirs present before (count)'
run 'docker compose -f docker-compose.yml --env-file .env.example config --format json | jq -r ".services|to_entries[]|\"\(.key) \(.value.image)\"" | sort  # the refs bundle.sh will save'
run "docker image inspect --format '{{index .RepoTags 0}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}} {{.Id}}' ke-app:u3win-98b6d27 ke-gateway:u3win-98b6d27"
run "docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E '^ke-(app|gateway):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/|^anchore/syft' | sort  # tags bundle.sh (re)points; restored after if moved"
docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E "^ke-(app|gateway):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/" | sort > "$S/tags-before.txt"
run 'docker ps -a --format "{{.Names}} {{.Label \"com.docker.compose.project\"}}" | grep -c "ke-tester-u3win"  # containers of ke-tester-u3win* projects (expect 0: stack not up yet)'

echo '--- freshness (security M-1): bundle.sh uses KE_IMAGE_TAG=u3win-98b6d27, the tag phase build just built from this tree, never local'
run "docker image inspect --format '{{index .RepoTags 0}} id={{.Id}} created={{.Created}} {{.Os}}/{{.Architecture}}' ke-app:u3win-98b6d27 ke-gateway:u3win-98b6d27 ke-app:local ke-gateway:local  # the stale :local pair shown for contrast only"
APPID="$(docker image inspect -f "{{.Id}}" ke-app:u3win-98b6d27)"; GWID="$(docker image inspect -f "{{.Id}}" ke-gateway:u3win-98b6d27)"
run "docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E ' ($APPID|$GWID)\$'  # every tag on the two fresh image IDs"
run "MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker run --rm --network none --pull never --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u3win-probe --entrypoint python ke-app:u3win-98b6d27 -I -c \"import importlib.util as u; print('psycopg_binary spec:', u.find_spec('psycopg_binary')); print('pip spec:', u.find_spec('pip')); import psycopg; print('psycopg impl:', psycopg.pq.__impl__, psycopg.pq.version())\""
run "MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker run --rm --network none --pull never --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u3win-probe --entrypoint sh ke-app:u3win-98b6d27 -c 'dpkg-query -W libpq5; ls -d /opt/venv/lib/python3.12/site-packages/psycopg_binary* 2>&1 | head -3'"
run "MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1 docker run --rm --network none --pull never --label com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u3win-probe --entrypoint python ke-app:local -I -c \"import importlib.util as u; print('psycopg_binary spec:', u.find_spec('psycopg_binary'))\"  # contrast: the stale :local the developer's bundle used"

echo '--- the run (full output; time in seconds after it)'
echo "\$ scripts/bundle.sh --out <scratch>/bundle-u3win-amd64"
t0=$(date +%s)
scripts/bundle.sh --out "$OUT" 2>&1 | red
rc=${PIPESTATUS[0]}
echo "[exit code: $rc] [elapsed: $(( $(date +%s) - t0 )) s]"
echo

echo '--- the six SBOMs (and the image tars they describe)'
run "cd \"$OUT\" && ls -la app/sboms && stat -c '%s bytes  %n' app/sboms/* && stat -c '%s bytes  %n' images/*"
run "cd \"$OUT\" && cat bundle.json"
run "cd \"$OUT\" && grep -c '' MANIFEST.sha256 && grep 'app/sboms/' MANIFEST.sha256"
run "cd \"$OUT\" && sha256sum -c --quiet MANIFEST.sha256 && echo 'MANIFEST.sha256: every file verifies'"
run "cat \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\"; (cd \"$S\" && sha256sum bundle-u3win-amd64/MANIFEST.sha256)"
run "cd \"$OUT\" && python -I -c 'import json,sys; [print(f, json.load(open(f))[\"spdxVersion\"]) for f in sys.argv[1:]]' app/sboms/*.spdx.json  # each parses as JSON"
run "\"$R/.venv/Scripts/python.exe\" -I \"$(cygpath -m "$S/tools/check_sboms.py")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$OUT")\" \"$(cygpath -m "$R")\"  # validate() per file, the tests' equivalent"

echo '--- after: nothing left by the run'
run 'docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u3win-sbom -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u3win-sbomcheck -q | wc -l; docker ps -a --filter label=com.docker.compose.project=ke-tester-u3win-notices -q | wc -l  # sbom, sbomcheck, notices containers (all --rm)'
run 'docker ps -a -q --filter label=com.docker.compose.service=sbom | wc -l  # sbom containers, any project'
run 'docker compose ls -a --filter name=ke-tester-u3win'
run 'docker network ls --filter name=ke-tester-u3win-sbom'
run 'ls -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}"/ke-sbom.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l  # work dirs left (trap removes; expect the same count as before)'
docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E "^ke-(app|gateway):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/" | sort > "$S/tags-after.txt"
run "diff \"$S/tags-before.txt\" \"$S/tags-after.txt\" && echo 'bundle and ke-vendor tags: unchanged'"
if ! diff -q "$S/tags-before.txt" "$S/tags-after.txt" > /dev/null; then
  echo '--- restoring moved tags to their earlier image IDs'
  while read -r t id; do run "docker tag $id $t"; done < "$S/tags-before.txt"
  comm -13 "$S/tags-before.txt" "$S/tags-after.txt" | while read -r t id; do grep -q "^$t " "$S/tags-before.txt" || run "docker image rm $t  # new tag from this run"; done
  run "docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E '^ke-(app|gateway):bundle-$SHA12 |^ke-vendor/' | sort | diff \"$S/tags-before.txt\" - && echo 'tags restored'"
fi

echo '--- keep the six SBOMs and bundle.json for the evidence regeneration (tester scratch dir <scratch>/u3win-sboms), then delete the scratch bundle'
run "rm -rf \"$S/u3win-sboms\" && mkdir \"$S/u3win-sboms\" && cp -- \"$OUT\"/app/sboms/*.spdx.json \"$OUT/bundle.json\" \"$S/u3win-sboms/\" && (cd \"$S/u3win-sboms\" && sha256sum *.spdx.json) && (cd \"$OUT\" && sha256sum app/sboms/*.spdx.json)"
run "rm -rf -- \"$OUT\" \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\"; ls -d \"$OUT\" \"$OUT.manifest.sha256\""
echo "[end 3b]"

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/final-check.sh ====================
#!/usr/bin/env bash
S=<scratch>
cd /c/repos/Knowledge-engine
c() { echo "\$ $1"; bash -c "$1" 2>&1; }
c 'date -u +%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ'
c 'docker ps -a --format "{{.Label \"com.docker.compose.project\"}}" | grep -c "ke-tester-u3win"  # containers of any ke-tester-u3win* project: stack, -sbom, -sbomcheck, -notices, -probe'
c 'docker network ls --filter name=ke-tester-u3win; docker volume ls --filter name=ke-tester-u3win'
c 'docker compose ls -a --filter name=ke-tester-u3win'
c 'docker image ls --filter reference="ke-*:u3win-*"'

c "docker image ls --no-trunc --format '{{.Repository}}:{{.Tag}} {{.ID}}' | grep -E '^ke-(app|gateway):bundle-98b6d27c1f73 |^ke-vendor/' | sort | diff \"$S/tags-before.txt\" - && echo 'bundle-98b6d27c1f73 and ke-vendor tags: as before section 3b'"
c "ls -d \"$S/bundle-u3win-amd64\" \"$S/bundle-u3win-amd64.manifest.sha256\" 2>&1 | sed 's#$S#<scratch>#g'  # scratch bundle and its record deleted"
c 'ls -d "${TMPDIR:-/tmp}"/ke-sbom.* 2>/dev/null | wc -l  # bundle.sh work dirs left'
c 'ls -d /c/ke-tester-u3win-sbom-dummy  # dummy bind source never created'
c 'ls -a .env  # repo root'
c 'git rev-parse HEAD; git branch --show-current'
c 'sha256sum docker/app.Dockerfile docker-compose.sbom.yml scripts/bundle.sh scripts/offline_check.sh scripts/sbom_check.py uv.lock pyproject.toml  # end; must equal 00-start and section 0'
c "sha256sum docker/app.Dockerfile docker-compose.sbom.yml scripts/bundle.sh scripts/offline_check.sh scripts/sbom_check.py uv.lock pyproject.toml | diff <(grep -A7 '(start)' \"$S/out/00-start.txt\" | tail -7) - && echo 'seven sha256: identical start and end'"
c 'git diff --stat HEAD -- scripts/*.ps1 docker-compose*.yml docker/; git status --short --untracked-files=all -- scripts/*.ps1 docker-compose*.yml docker/'
c 'KE_SBOM_IN=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_OUT=/nonexistent KE_SBOM_USER=1000:1000 docker compose --env-file .env.example -f docker-compose.sbom.yml config --format json | python -I -c "import json,sys; d=json.load(sys.stdin); s=d[\"services\"][\"sbom\"]; print(\"services.sbom keys:\", sorted(s)); print(\"user:\", s.get(\"user\")); print(\"top-level keys:\", sorted(d)); print(\"absent:\", {k: k not in s for k in (\"ports\",\"profiles\",\"networks\",\"privileged\",\"cap_add\",\"devices\",\"pid\",\"ipc\")})"'
c 'git status --short'
echo '[end]'

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/tools/check_sboms.py ====================
"""Tester helper (U3 Windows proof): checks the six SBOMs a real bundle.sh run wrote, the way
tests/test_sbom_bundle.py does: loads scripts/sbom_check.py by path and calls validate(doc), then
checks name == bundle.json ref, the ke:ref/ke:arch/ke:image-config annotations (image-config
against sbom_check.config_digest of the saved tar), distinct urn:uuid namespaces, and that each
file's sha256 is the one in MANIFEST.sha256. argv: <bundle dir> <repo root>. Exit 1 on any problem."""
import hashlib, importlib.util, json, pathlib, sys

out, repo = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]), pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2])
spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("ke_sbom_check_under_test", repo / "scripts" / "sbom_check.py")
m = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
spec.loader.exec_module(m)
bundle = json.loads((out / "bundle.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
manifest = dict(reversed(l.split("  ", 1)) for l in (out / "MANIFEST.sha256").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines())
present = sorted(p.name for p in (out / "app" / "sboms").iterdir())
expected = sorted(pathlib.PurePosixPath(i["file"]).stem + ".spdx.json" for i in bundle["images"])
bad = 0
print(f"bundle.json arch={bundle['arch']} git_sha={bundle['git_sha']} dirty={bundle['dirty']} images={len(bundle['images'])}")
print(f"app/sboms/: {present}")
print(f"one per bundle.json image: {present == expected}")
bad += present != expected
spaces = []
for i in bundle["images"]:
    name = pathlib.PurePosixPath(i["file"]).stem
    rel = f"app/sboms/{name}.spdx.json"
    raw = (out / rel).read_bytes()
    doc = json.loads(raw)
    probs = m.validate(doc)
    ann = {}
    for a in doc.get("annotations") or []:
        c = str(a.get("comment", ""))
        if c.startswith("ke:") and "=" in c:
            k, v = c[3:].split("=", 1)
            ann[k] = v
    cfg = m.config_digest(str(out / i["file"]))
    pkgs = doc.get("packages") or []
    pypi = sum(1 for p in pkgs for r in p.get("externalRefs") or [] if str(r.get("referenceLocator", "")).startswith("pkg:pypi/"))
    checks = {
        "validate()==[]": probs == [],
        "spdxVersion SPDX-2.3": doc.get("spdxVersion") == "SPDX-2.3",
        "name==ref": doc.get("name") == i["ref"],
        "ke:ref==ref": ann.get("ref") == i["ref"],
        "ke:arch==bundle arch": ann.get("arch") == bundle["arch"],
        "ke:image-config==tar config": ann.get("image-config") == cfg,
        "manifest sha256": manifest.get(rel) == hashlib.sha256(raw).hexdigest(),
    }
    ok = all(checks.values())
    bad += not ok
    spaces.append(doc.get("documentNamespace"))
    print(f"\n{rel}: {len(raw)} bytes; {'OK' if ok else 'PROBLEM'}")
    print(f"  ref {i['ref']}; services {i['services']}")
    print(f"  namespace {doc.get('documentNamespace')}; packages {len(pkgs)} (pkg:pypi purls {pypi}); files {len(doc.get('files') or [])}; relationships {len(doc.get('relationships') or [])}")
    print(f"  creators {doc.get('creationInfo', {}).get('creators')}")
    print(f"  ke:image-config {ann.get('image-config')}")
    print("  " + "; ".join(f"{k} {v}" for k, v in checks.items()))
    if probs:
        print("  validate problems:", probs[:10])
distinct = len(set(spaces)) == len(spaces)
bad += not distinct
print(f"\nnamespaces distinct across the bundle: {distinct} ({len(set(spaces))}/{len(spaces)})")
print("RESULT:", "PASS" if bad == 0 else f"FAIL ({bad} problem(s))")
sys.exit(1 if bad else 0)

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/tools/sbom_evidence.py ====================
"""Tester helper: writes <image>.pkgs.tsv (purl, version, declared|concluded; document order; '-' when no purl)
from an SBOM, and the gpl-family-packages.md body from the TSVs. Subcommands: tsv <spdx.json> | md <tsv dir> [header line...]"""
import json, pathlib, re, sys

NOLIC = {"", "NOASSERTION", "NONE"}

def tsv(path):
    doc = json.loads(pathlib.Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    out = []
    for p in doc.get("packages") or []:
        purl = next((r.get("referenceLocator") for r in p.get("externalRefs") or [] if r.get("referenceType") == "purl"), None) or "-"
        out.append(f"{purl}\t{p.get('versionInfo', '')}\t{p.get('licenseDeclared', '')}|{p.get('licenseConcluded', '')}")
    return "\n".join(out) + "\n"

def rows(tsv_path):
    for line in tsv_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
        purl, version, lic = line.split("\t")
        declared, concluded = lic.split("|", 1)
        yield purl, version, declared, concluded

def classify(declared, concluded, mode):
    gpl = "GPL" in declared or "GPL" in concluded
    if mode == "both":
        nolic = declared in NOLIC and concluded in NOLIC
    else:
        nolic = declared in NOLIC or concluded in NOLIC
    return gpl, nolic

def table(items):
    return ["| purl | version | declared / concluded |", "|---|---|---|"] + [f"| {p} | {v} | {d} / {c} |" for p, v, d, c in items]

def md(d, mode="both"):
    d = pathlib.Path(d)
    lines = []
    stats = {}
    for t in sorted(d.glob("*.pkgs.tsv")):
        name = t.name[: -len(".pkgs.tsv")]
        all_rows = list(rows(t))
        gpl = [r for r in all_rows if classify(r[2], r[3], mode)[0]]
        nol = [r for r in all_rows if classify(r[2], r[3], mode)[1] and not classify(r[2], r[3], mode)[0]]
        stats[name] = (len(all_rows), len(gpl), len(nol), all_rows, gpl, nol)
        lines += [f"## {name}: {len(all_rows)} packages, GPL-family {len(gpl)}, no licence/NOASSERTION {len(nol)}", "",
                  f"### GPL-family ({len(gpl)})", ""] + table(gpl) + ["", f"### No licence / NOASSERTION ({len(nol)})", ""] + table(nol) + [""]
    return lines, stats

if __name__ == "__main__":
    if sys.argv[1] == "tsv":
        sys.stdout.write(tsv(sys.argv[2]))
    elif sys.argv[1] == "md":
        lines, _ = md(sys.argv[2], sys.argv[3] if len(sys.argv) > 3 else "both")
        sys.stdout.write("\n".join(lines))

==================== Tester file: <scratch>/tools/write_gpl_md.py ====================
"""Tester helper: assemble gpl-family-packages.md from a TSV dir. argv: <tsv dir> <images header line> <out md>
Writes CRLF, as the developer's file (Python text-mode on Windows)."""
import pathlib, sys
sys.path.insert(0, str(pathlib.Path(__file__).parent))
import sbom_evidence as se

CRLF = chr(13) + chr(10)
d, header, outp = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]), sys.argv[2], pathlib.Path(sys.argv[3])
body, stats = se.md(d, "both")
neo = stats["neo4j"][3]
maven = [r for r in neo if r[0].startswith("pkg:maven/")]
own = lambda p: p.startswith(("pkg:maven/org.neo4j/", "pkg:maven/org.neo4j.", "pkg:maven/com.neo4j/", "pkg:maven/com.neo4j."))
third = [r for r in maven if not own(r[0])]
tgpl = [r for r in third if "GPL" in r[2] or "GPL" in r[3]]
tnol = [r for r in third if r[2] in se.NOLIC and r[3] in se.NOLIC]
jar = ["## neo4j maven jars outside Neo4j's own groups (ruling 5)", "",
       f"Of neo4j's {len(maven)} maven packages, {len(maven) - len(third)} are in Neo4j's own groups (org.neo4j*, com.neo4j*) and {len(third)} are third-party. "
       f"Third-party GPL-family: {len(tgpl)}; third-party with no licence (declared and concluded both empty/NOASSERTION/NONE): {len(tnol)}.", "",
       f"### Third-party GPL-family ({len(tgpl)})", ""] + se.table(tgpl) + ["", f"### Third-party no licence / NOASSERTION ({len(tnol)})", ""] + se.table(tnol) + [""]
lines = ["# GPL-family and unlicensed packages per image (U3, real syft v1.54.1 SBOMs; regenerated by the tester from a fresh bundle, security M-1)",
         header,
         "Selection: licenseConcluded or licenseDeclared contains GPL, LGPL or AGPL in any form, or is empty/NOASSERTION/NONE. Source: `sboms/<image>.pkgs.tsv` (lists from the real `app/sboms/<image>.spdx.json`).",
         ""] + body + jar
outp.write_bytes(CRLF.join(lines).encode("utf-8"))
for n, s in stats.items():
    print(f"{n}: {s[0]} packages, GPL-family {s[1]}, no licence/NOASSERTION {s[2]}")
print(f"neo4j maven {len(maven)}, own {len(maven) - len(third)}, third-party {len(third)}, third-party GPL-family {[(r[0], r[2] + ' / ' + r[3]) for r in tgpl]}, third-party no licence {len(tnol)}")
